Comments 
Standard Criteria 
61.1 Perform new -
born 
Resuscitation 
 
61.1.1 Call for help     
61.1.2 Take the baby to the warm 
resuscitation table     
61.1.3 Dry the baby with clean and dry 
cloth      
61.1.4 Position the head of the baby in 
the neutral position to open the 
airway, 
 
 
  
61.1.5 Clear airway, if necessary     
61.1.6 Stimulate and reposition the baby     

===== PAGE 425 =====

Practicum Guide for Tutors and Clinical Instructors  
NTA Level 5Clinical Medicine   425 
 
 
 
61.1.7 Give oxygen as necessary     
61.1.8 Use a correctly fitting mask and 
give the bab y 5 slow ventilations with 
ambu bag. 
 
 
  
61.1.9 Observe closely if the baby 
breathing     
61.1.10 If not breathing check position and 
mask fit (adjust position, if necessary)      
61.1.11 Provide ventilation with bag and 
mask     
61.1.12 If chest not moving well suc k the 
airway     
61.1.13 Check the heart rate (HR) (cord 
pulsation or by listening with 
stethoscope) 
 
 
  
61.1.14 Compress the chest if 
respiratory rate is less than 60 
breath per minute 
 
 
  

===== PAGE 426 =====

Practicum Guide for Tutors and Clinical Instructors  
NTA Level 5Clinical Medicine   426 
 
 
 
61.1.15 Continue to bag at a rate of 
about 40 breaths per minute.     
61.1.16 Make sure the chest is mo ving 
adequately.     
61.1.17 Use oxygen if available every 1 –
2 minutes stop and see if the 
pulse or breathing has improved. 
 
 
  
61.1.18 Stop compressions once the 
heart rate is more than 
100b/min. 
 
 
  
61.1.19 Continue oxygen until pink and 
active

SESSION CONTENT 
 
Step 1: Presentation of Session Title and Learning 
Outcomes (5 minutes) 
 
READ or ASK students to read the learning objectives 
and clarify. 
 
ASK students if they have any questions before 
continuing. 
 
EXPLAIN to participants that this session will take 120 
minutes 
 
Step 2: Introduction, Definition of Terms and Purpose 
of Practicum Guides (15 minutes) 
 
Introduction 
There are three Practicum Guides handbooks for each 
NTA level: 
 A Practicum Guide for NTA Level 4 
 A Practicum Guide for NTA Level 5 
 A Practicum Guide for NTA Level 6 
 
Practicum guides contain practicum for all modules with 
practical hours.  
 
 
 
  

===== PAGE 430 =====

Practicum Guide for Tutors and Clinical Instructors  
NTA Level 5Clinical Medicine   430 
 
 
 
Definition of terms: 
 
Activity:Brainstorming (10 minutes) 
 
ASK students, “What is a  practicum, Rotation, Placement, 
Community fieldwork, Field Visit? 
 
ALLOW for a few responses. 
 
WRITE on chalk board or flip charts 
 
DISTRIBUTE copies of practicum guides to all the students and 
ask them to open the introduction pages where they can find 
the definition of the above terms. 
 
SUMMARISE using the contents below 
 
 Practicum  
o Refers to the part of a course consisting of practical 
work in a particular field. 
o The new curriculum for NTA level 4, 5 and 6 
stresses on competency-based training; it was 
found necessary to standardize both the classroom 
and the clinical training. 
o This overview attempts to create some guidance on 
how training should be standardized in the clinic 
and other settings where students will be attached. 
 
 Rotation/ Placement 
o A period in whi ch students in the clinical part of 
their education passes through various clinical 

===== PAGE 431 =====

Practicum Guide for Tutors and Clinical Instructors  
NTA Level 5Clinical Medicine   431 
 
 
 
areas for short periods of time so that they have 
opportunity to learn the different aspects of care. 
 
 Field Visit 
o A field visit is an off -school educational experience 
to students which normally involves travelling to a 
place where the students will acquire intended 
knowledge, experience and skills.  
o The field visit is intended to give an opportunity to 
students to experience the actual situation. The 
field visit usually la sts from a few hours to a day or 
days. 
 Community Field Work 
o A community fieldwork is an off -school educational 
experience to students which normally involves 
travelling to a selected community setting. 
o Community field work aims to provide students with 
opportunities to work at a community level for 
health promotion, so that they can gain experience 
with assessing community needs, planning with the 
community, implementing the plan, and evaluating 
progress with the community.  
 
Purpose of Practicum Guide 
 Give guidance to tutors, clinical instructors and 
students on clinical teaching and learning 
 Provide standardised clinical/practical learning 
activities across NTA Leve 5 
 Improve the quality of teaching and learning in 
clinical/practical settings 

===== PAGE 432 =====

Practicum Guide for Tutors and Clinical Instructors  
NTA Level 5Clinical Medicine   432 
 
 
 
 Monitor stude nts learning in clinical/practical areas 
effectively

Practicum Guide for Tutors and Clinical Instructors

Step 7: How to Use the Practicum Guide (5 minutes) 
 Each learner will get a copy of the practicum guide. 
 Each learner has to understand all the activities and 
assignments intended in each clinical rotation for the 
purpose of learning and practicing skills.  
 Pay close attention to: clinical obje ctives, assignments, 
monitoring and evaluation tools (checklists)  
 Make sure that each learner refers to a relevant 
practicum while in clinical rotation on daily basis. 
 Look at the specific area of practice and review skills 
needed 
 Review the clinical/prac tical outcomes, competencies 
and the assignments/activities 
 Go to clinical/practical area with some specific activity 
or assignment you want to learn 
 
Step 8: Key Points (5 minutes) 
 The Practicum Guide contains clinical/practical 
teaching materials for lea rning in clinical/practical 
areas  
 All clinical modules and community field work  are 
included in this practicum guide 
 Tutors, clinical instructors, hospital staff (non -teaching 
staff), students and patients each have an important 
role in the practicum 
 It i s very important for tutors, clinical instructors and 
students to read and utilize the Practicum Guide
